   Impairment of financial assets                                                  金融資產減值
   The Group recognizes an allowance for expected credit losses                    本集團確認對並非按公平值計入損益的
   (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through                所有債務工具預期信貸虧損（「預期信
   profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the                    貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基
   contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract                      於根據合約到期的合約現金流量與本集
   and all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive,                       團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額
   discounted at an approximation of the original effective interest               而釐定、以原實際利率的近似值貼現。
   rate. The expected cash flows will include cash flows from the                  預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的現
   sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are                   金流量或組成合約條款的其他信貸提升
   integral to the contractual terms.                                              措施。

   General approach                                                                普通法
   ECLs are recognized in two stages. For credit exposures for                     預期信貸虧損分兩個階段進行確認。就
   which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk                  自初始確認起信貸風險並無大幅增加的
   since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses                  信貸敞口而言，會就未來12個月內可能
   that result from default events that are possible within the next               發生違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12個
   12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for                      月預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。
   which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since                就自初始確認起信貸風險大幅增加的該
   initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses             等信貸敞口而言，不論何時發生違約，
   expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective                  於敞口的餘下年期內的預期信貸虧損均
   of the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                  須計提虧損撥備（全期預期信貸虧損）。

   At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit                   於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具信
   risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since                貸風險是否自初始確認起大幅增加。於
   initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group                      進行評估時，本集團比較金融工具於報
   compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial                       告日期發生違約的風險及金融工具於初
   instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default                  始確認日期發生違約的風險，認為在無
   occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial                 需付出過多成本或努力的情況下即可獲
   recognition and considers reasonable and supportable                            得合理可靠的資料（包括歷史及前瞻性
   information that is available without undue cost or effort,                     資料）。
   including historical and forward-looking information.

   For debt investments at fair value through other comprehensive                  就按公平值計入其他全面收益的債務投
   income, the Group applies the low credit risk simplification.                   資 而 言， 本 集 團 採 用 低 信 貸 風 險 簡 化
   At each reporting date, the Group evaluates whether the                         法。於各報告日期，本集團毋須花費不
   debt investments are considered to have low credit risk using                   必要之成本或努力，即可使用所有合理
   all reasonable and supportable information that is available                    可靠資料評估債務投資是否被視為低信
   without undue cost or effort. In making that evaluation, the                    貸風險。在進行評估時，本集團會重新
   Group reassesses the external credit ratings of the debt                        評估債務投資的外部信用評級。此外，
   investments. In addition, the Group considers that there has                    本集團認為合約付款逾期超過30日時，

   Simplified approach                                                               簡化法
   For trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing                 就並無重大融資成分的貿易應收款項或
   component or when the Group applies the practical expedient                       本集團就任何重大融資成分應用不調整
   of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component,                 交易價格的可行權宜方法而言，本集團
   the Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs.                    採用簡化法計算預期信貸虧損。根據簡
   Under the simplified approach, the Group does not track                           化法，本集團並未追踪信貸風險變動，
   changes in credit risk, but instead recognizes a loss allowance                   轉而於各報告日期根據全期預期信貸虧
   based on lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group has                      損確認虧損撥備。本集團已根據其歷史
   established a provision matrix that is based on its historical                    信貸虧損經驗建立撥備矩陣，並就債務
   credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors                      人及經濟環境特定的前瞻性因素作出調
   specific to the debtors and the economic environment.                             整。

   Financial liabilities                                                             金融負債

   borrowings and payables.                                                          付款項。

   All financial liabilities are recognized initially at fair value and,             所有金融負債初始按公平值確認，貸款
   in the case of loans and borrowings and payables, net of                          及借貸以及應付款項則須扣除直接應佔
   directly attributable transaction costs.                                          交易成本。

   The Group’s financial liabilities include trade payables, financial               本集團的金融負債包括貿易應付款項及

2.4 Summary of Significant Accounting Policies                                2.4 重大會計政策概要（續）
    (continued)

   Derecognition of financial liabilities                                          終止確認金融負債
   A financial liability is derecognized when the obligation under                 金融負債於負債的責任解除、註銷或屆
   the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                           滿時終止確認。

   When an existing financial liability is replaced by another                     當現有金融負債被同一貸款人以條款明
   from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the                   顯 不 同 的 負 債 取 代 時， 或 現 有 負 債 的
   terms of an existing liability are substantially modified, such                 條款有重大修改時，有關替換或修改則
   an exchange or modification is treated as a derecognition of                    視為終止確認原有負債並確認一項新負
   the original liability and a recognition of a new liability, and                債， 而 相 關 賬 面 值 的 差 額 於 損 益 表 確
   the difference between the respective carrying amounts is                       認。
   recognized in the statement of profit or loss.

   Offsetting of financial instruments                                             抵銷金融工具
   Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net               倘（且僅當）有現行可強制執行的法律
   amount is reported in the statement of financial position if,                   權利抵銷已確認金額，並且擬以淨額基
   and only if, there is a currently enforceable legal right to offset             準進行結算或同時將資產變現及清償負
   the recognized amounts and there is an intention to settle on                   債，方可將金融資產及金融負債抵銷，
   a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle the liabilities                淨額於財務狀況表呈報。
   simultaneously.

   Inventories                                                                     存貨
   Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable                  存貨按成本及可變現淨值兩者中的較低
   value. Cost is determined on the weighted average basis, and                    者 列 賬。 成 本 按 加 權 平 均 基 準 釐 定，
   in the case of work in progress and finished goods, comprises                   而就在製品及製成品而言，成本包括直
   direct materials, direct labour and an appropriate proportion of                接材料、直接勞工及適當比例的生產費
   overheads. Net realisable value is based on estimated selling                   用。可變現淨值為估計售價減去任何在
   prices less any estimated costs to be incurred to completion                    完成及出售過程中產生的估計費用。
   and disposal.

3. Significant Accounting Estimates and                                       3. 重要會計估計及假設
   Assumptions

   The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires                    管理層在編製本集團財務報表時須作出
   management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions                        判斷、估計及假設，而有關判斷、估計
   that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets                  及假設會對收入、開支、資產及負債的
   and liabilities, and their accompanying disclosures, and the                    呈報金額及其隨附披露，以及或然負債
   disclosure of contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these                   披露造成影響。該等假設及估計的不確
   assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that could                   定性或會導致須對在未來遭受影響的資
   require a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of the                    產或負債的賬面值進行重大調整。
   assets or liabilities affected in the future.

   Judgements                                                                      判斷
   In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,                     於採納本集團會計政策的過程中，除涉
   management has made the following judgements, apart from                        及估計的會計政策外，管理層已作出對
   those involving estimations, which have the most significant                    財務報表已確認金額構成最重大影響的
   effect on the amounts recognized in the financial statements:                   判斷如下：

   Property lease classification – Group as lessor                                 物業租賃分類 － 本集團作為出租人
   The Group has entered into commercial property leases on                        本集團訂立有關投資物業組合的商務物
   its investment property portfolio. The Group has determined,                    業租賃。根據對有關安排條款及條件之
   based on an evaluation of the terms and conditions of the                       評估，如租期不構成商用物業之經濟壽
   arrangements, such as the lease term not constituting a major                   命的主要部分且最低租賃款項現值不等
   part of the economic life of the commercial property and the                    於商用物業之絕大部分公平值，本集團
   present value of the minimum lease payments not amounting                       已釐定其保留該等物業所有權附帶的絕
   to substantially all the fair value of the commercial property,                 大部分風險及回報，並將有關合約以經
   that it retains substantially all the significant risks and rewards             營租賃入賬。
   incidental to ownership of these properties which are leased
   out and accounts for the contracts as operating leases.

   Classification between investment property and owner-occupied                   投資物業與自用物業的分類
   property
   The Group determines whether a property qualifies as an                         本集團釐定物業是否符合投資物業的資
   investment property, and has developed criteria in making                       格， 並 已 制 訂 作 出 此 判 斷 的 準 則。 投
   that judgement. Investment property is a property held to                       資物業是為賺取租金或資本增值或同時
   earn rentals or for capital appreciation or both. Therefore, the                為此兩項目的而持有的物業。因此，本
   Group considers whether a property generates cash flows                         集團會考慮一項物業產生現金流量時是
   largely independently of the other assets held by the Group.                    否基本上與本集團所持有的其他資產無
   Some property comprises a portion that is held to earn rentals                  關。某些物業的部份是為賺取租金或資
   or for capital appreciation and another portion that is held                    本增值而持有，而物業內的另一些部份
   for use in the production or supply of goods or services or                     是為用於生產或供應貨品或服務或作行
   for administrative purposes. If these portions could be sold                    政用途而持有。倘若此等部份可以分開
   separately or leased out separately under a finance lease,                      出售或根據融資租賃分開出租，本集團
   the Group accounts for the portions separately. If the portions                 會將有關部份分開入賬。倘若該等部份
   could not be sold separately, the property is an investment                     無法分開出售，則只會在用於生產或供
   property only if an insignificant portion is held for use in the                應貨品或服務或作行政用途而持有的部
   production or supply of goods or services or for administrative                 份並不重要時，有關物業才會列作投資
   purposes. Judgement is made on an individual property basis                     物業。本集團對各項物業作出判斷，以
   to determine whether ancillary services are so significant that                 決定配套服務是否重要以使物業並不符
   a property does not qualify as an investment property.                          合投資物業的資格。

      Fair value hierarchy (continued)                                          公平值層級（續）
      Under the term and reversion method, fair value is measured               根據年期及復歸法，公平值乃經計及現
      by taking into account the rental income derived from the                 有租約產生的租金收入，並就該等租約
      existing lease with due allowance for the reversionary income             的潛在可復歸收入計提適當撥備，其後
      potential of the lease, which is then capitalised into the value          按適用資本化比率撥充資本計算價值而
      at appropriate rates.                                                     達致。

      The term value was estimated based on the existing rent, the              定期價值乃根據租賃協議所訂明的現有
      tenancy period as stated in the lease agreement and the yield             租金、租期及年期內（租期）回報率估
      during the term period (tenancy period). The reversionary                 計。復歸價值乃根據市值租金、市場回
      value was estimated based on the market rents, the market                 報率及復歸期估計。
      yield and the reversionary periods.

      A significant increase (decrease) in the estimated rental value           估計租賃價值及市場租金年增長率單獨
      and the market rent growth rate per annum in isolation would              大幅上升（下降）將導致投資物業的公
      result in a significant increase (decrease) in the fair value of the      平 值 大 幅 增 加（減 少）。 長 期 空 置 率 及
      investment property. A significant increase (decrease) in the             貼現率單獨大幅上升（下降）將導致投
      long term vacancy rate and the discount rate in isolation would           資 物 業 的 公 平 值 大 幅 減 少（增 加）。 一
      result in a significant decrease (increase) in the fair value of          般而言，就估計租賃價值作出的假設變
      the investment property. Generally, a change in the assumption            動同時，租金年增長率及貼現率出現同
      made for the estimated rental value is accompanied by a                   向變動，而長期空置率出現反向變動。
      directionally similar change in the rent growth per annum and
      the discount rate and an opposite change in the long term
      vacancy rate.

      prescribed by HKFRS 9, which permits the use of the lifetime          明 計 算 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 之 簡 化 方 法， 該
      expected loss for all trade receivables. To measure the               準則允許就所有貿易應收款項採用整個
      expected credit losses, trade receivables have been grouped           存續期的預期虧損。為計量預期信貸虧
      based on shared credit risk characteristics and the days              損，貿易應收款項已根據共同信貸風險
      past due. The expected credit losses have also incorporated           特性及逾期天數進行分組。預期信貸虧
      forward-looking information. The expected credit loss rates for       損亦包含前瞻性資料。逾期或于三個月
      trade receivables that were not yet past due or aged within 3         內到期的貿易應收款項的預期信貸損失
      months are minimal.                                                   率極低。

      The Group applies a general approach in calculating ECLs              本集團採用一般方法計算應收票據的預
      for notes receivable. All of the notes receivable are not past        期 信 貸 虧 損。 所 有 應 收 票 據 均 為 未 逾
      due. The Group classified such instruments as Stage 1 and             期。 本 集 團 將 此 類 工 具 分 類 為 第 一 階
      measured ECLs on a 12-month basis. However, when there                段，並按十二個月預期信貸虧損基準計
      has been a significant increase in credit risk since initial          量。然而，自發生以來信貸風險顯著增
      recognition, the allowance will be based on the lifetime ECL.         加時，撥備將以年限內預期信貸虧損為
      For bank acceptance bills, as the relevant financial institutions     基準。就銀行承兌票據而言，由於相關

      past due, loss rate is expected to be minimal as well.                言，預計損失率亦將極低。

      directors, including a non-executive director, an executive         與者包括本公司董事（包括一名非執行
      director and other employees of the Group. The share options,       董事）及本集團其他僱員。除非另被取
      unless otherwise cancelled or amended, will remain in force for     消或修訂，該等購股權將由各生效日期
      7 years from the respective effective date.                         起計7年內有效。

31. Share Option Scheme (continued)                                       31. 購股權計劃（續）

   The exercise price of share options is determinable by the                  購股權之行使價為由董事釐定，惟不得
   directors, but may not be less than the higher of (i) the Stock             少於以下兩者之最高者：(i)本公司股份
   Exchange closing price of the Company’s shares on the                       於授予購股權日期在聯交所之收市價；
   date of offer of the share options; and (ii) the average Stock              及(ii)本公司股份於緊接授出日期前五個
   Exchange closing price of the Company’s shares for the five                 交易日在聯交所之平均收市價。

      According to the relevant PRC laws and regulations for                   根據中國有關外商獨資企業的法律及法
      wholly-foreign-owned enterprises, which are applicable to                規（適 用 於 本 公 司 的 中 國 子 公 司）， 按
      the Company’s subsidiaries in the PRC, profits of the PRC                照適用的中國會計規則及規例釐定的中
      subsidiaries, as determined in accordance with the applicable            國子公司的溢利，可於中國子公 司(1)
      accounting rules and regulations in the PRC, are available               償 付 所 有 稅 務 負 債；(2)就 過 往 年 度 虧
      for distribution in the form of cash dividends to the Company            損 作 出 撥 備； 及(3)就 法 定 儲 備 金 作 出
      after the PRC subsidiaries have (1) satisfied all tax liabilities;       撥備後以現金股息形式向本公司作出分
      (2) provided for losses in previous years; and (3) made                  派。本公司的中國子公司須劃撥不少於
      appropriations to the statutory reserve funds. The Company’s             其除稅後溢利的10%作為法定儲備金，
      subsidiaries in the PRC are required to appropriate not less             直至儲備結餘達到其註冊資本的50%。
      than 10% of their profit after taxation to the statutory reserve
      funds until the reserve balance reaches 50% of their registered
      capital.

   included at the amount at which the instrument could be                         願方交易（而非被迫或清盤出售）中可

   other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The following                       乃使用以下的方法及假設：
   methods and assumptions were used to estimate the fair

   through other comprehensive income have been estimated                          本投資之公平值採用市場法估算。倘最

   the unlisted investments, the fair values are estimated based                   基於交易價估算。倘並無該等交易可供
   on the dealing price. If there is no such deal to be referenced,                參考，董事將根據行業、規模、影響力
   the directors will determine comparable public companies                        及 策 略 釐 定 可 比 較 上 市 公 司（同 行），
   (peers) based on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and                     且就已確定的各可資比較公司計算適當
   to calculate an appropriate price multiple, such as price to                    的價格倍數，如市賬率（「市賬率」）倍
   book value (“P/B”) multiple, for each comparable company                        數。該倍數乃按可資比較公司之企業價
   identified. The multiple is calculated by dividing the enterprise               值除以淨資產所得。交易倍數隨後根據
   value of the comparable company by net assets. The trading                      公司特定情況及狀況就非流動性等因素
   multiple is then discounted for considerations such as                          予以貼現。貼現倍數適用於非上市股本
   illiquidity based on company-specific facts and circumstances.                  投資的相應淨資產以計量公平值。董事
   The discounted multiple is applied to the corresponding net                     認為，計入綜合財務狀況表的估值技術
   assets of the unlisted equity investments to measure the fair                   所產生的估計公平值以及計入其他全面
   value. The directors believe that the estimated fair values                     收益的公平值相關變動屬合理，而於報

38. Financial Risk Management Objectives and                                38.     財務風險管理目標及政策
    Policies

   The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise bank                    本集團的主要金融工具包括銀行貸
   loans, cash and short term deposits. The main purpose of                     款、 現 金 及 短 期 存 款。 該 等 金 融 工 具
   these financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s              的主要用途乃為本集團的營運籌措資
   operations. The Group has various other financial assets and                 金。本集團有多種直接自其營運產生的
   liabilities, such as trade and other receivables and trade and               其他金融資產及負債，如貿易及其他應
   other payables, which arise directly from its operations.                    收款項以及貿易及其他應付款項。

   The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments                本集團金融工具所產生的主要風險為外
   are foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The               幣風險、信用風險及流動資金風險。董
   board of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing                  事會檢討及協定管理各類該等風險的政
   each of these risks and they are summarised below.                           策，有關政策概述於下文。

   Foreign currency risk                                                        外幣風險
   The Group has transactional currency exposures. These                        本集團面臨交易外匯風險。該等風險產
   exposures arise from sales or purchases by its significant                   生自其在中國內地經營的重要子公司以
   subsidiary operating in Mainland China in US$ other than the                 美元進行的買賣，而並非以其功能貨幣
   subsidiary’s functional currency, which is RMB. During the year,             人民幣進行。於年內，本集團的銷售額
   approximately 41% (2018: 43%) of the Group’s sales were                      中約41%（二零一八年：43%）乃以進
   denominated in currencies other than the functional currency                 行銷售的子公司的功能貨幣以外的貨幣

38. Financial Risk Management Objectives and                             38. 財 務 風 險 管 理 目 標 及 政 策
    Policies (continued)                                                   （續）

      Credit risk                                                          信用風險
      Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not meet its        信用風險指對手方無法履行金融工具或
      obligations under a financial instrument or customer contract,       客戶合約項下責任而引致金融損失之風
      leading to a financial loss. The Group is exposed to credit risk     險。本集團面臨來自其經營活動（主要
      from its operating activities (primarily trade receivables).         為貿易應收款項）的信用風險。

      The Group trades only with recognized and creditworthy third         本集團僅與獲認可及信譽良好的第三方
      parties and related parties. It is the Group’s policy that all       及關聯方交易。本集團的政策規定，所
      customers who wish to trade on credit terms are subject to           有擬按信用方式交易的客戶均須通過信
      credit verification procedures. In addition, receivable balances     用核實程序。此外，由於持續對應收款
      are monitored on an ongoing basis and the Group’s exposure           項結餘進行監控，故本集團的壞賬風險
      to bad debts is not significant.                                     並不重大。

      The carrying amounts of cash and cash equivalents, trade and         綜合財務狀況表內現金及現金等價物、
      notes receivables, financial assets included in prepayments,         貿易應收款項及應收票據及計入預付款
      other receivables and other assets, amounts due from related         項、其他應收款項及其他資產的金融資
      parties and restricted and time deposits included in the             產、應收關聯方款項以及已凍結及定期
      consolidated statement of financial position represent the           存款的賬面值為本集團就其金融資產所
      Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to its           面臨的最大信用風險。本集團並無其他
      financial assets. The Group has no other financial assets which      存在重大信用風險的金融資產。
      carry significant exposure to credit risk.

      Further qualitative and quantitative information regarding           有關貿易應收款項的進一步定性及定量
      trade receivables, for which the Group applies the simplified        資料（本集團根據香港財務報告準則第
      approach in calculating ECLs under HKFRS 9, is disclosed in          9號對其採用簡化方法計算預期信貸虧
      note 21 to the financial statements.                                 損）於財務報表附註21內披露。

      All the carrying amounts of financial assets at amortised cost,      除 財 務 報 表 附 註22內 所 載 分 類 為 第 三
      applying the general approach under HKFRS 9, are classified          階段的全數已減值的其他應收款項，所
      as Stage 1 in terms of ECLs as at 31 December 2019 in                有按攤銷成本計量的金融資產的賬面值
      addition to the fully impaired other receivables classified as       （根據香港財務報告準則第9號採用一般
      Stage 3, as stated in note 22 to the financial statements.           方法）就二零一九年十二月三十一日的
                                                                           預期信貸虧損分類為第一階段。

      Capital management                                                  資本管理
      The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are        本集團資本管理的首要目標為，維護本
      to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern     集團的持續經營能力及維持良好的資本
      and to maintain healthy capital ratios to support its business      比率，以支持其業務及實現股東價值最
      and maximise shareholders’ value.                                   大化。

      The Group manages its capital structure and makes                   本集團根據經濟狀況的變動管理及調整
      adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions.       其資本架構。為維持或調整資本架構，
      To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may          本集團或會調整派付予股東的股息、向
      adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to      股東返還資本或發行新股。本集團不受
      shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to       任 何 外 部 施 加 的 資 本 要 求 規 限。 於 年
      any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were        內，本集團管理資本的目標、政策或程
      made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing          序概無任何變動。
      capital during the year.

      The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net      本集團以資產負債比率監控資本，資產
      debt divided by total equity plus net debt. The Group includes,     負債比率按淨負債除以權益總額加淨負
      within net debt, trade payables, other payables and accruals,       債計算。本集團將貿易應付款項、其他
      interest-bearing bank borrowings, lease liabilities and amounts     應付款項及暫估費用、計息銀行借款、
      due to related parties, less cash and cash equivalents. The         租賃負債以及應付關聯方款項扣除現金
      gearing ratios as at the end of each of the reporting periods       及現金等價物計入淨負債。各報告期末
      were as follows:                                                    的資產負債比率如下：
